# Evidence ledger: fc_uk_homesales_2026_09

- Question: Assume today is 2026-10-28. You know nothing published after this date. Estimate the number of UK residential property transactions (with a value of £40,000 or above) in September 2026, seasonally adjusted, as it will be first reported (provisional estimate) in HM Revenue & Customs' monthly release 'Monthly property transactions completed in the UK with value of £40,000 or above'.
- as_of: 2026-10-28
- Pinned target: Number of UK residential property transactions with value £40,000 or above, completed in September 2026, seasonally adjusted, as first provisionally reported by HMRC in its monthly 'Monthly property transactions completed in the UK with value of £40,000 or above' release | unit transactions | period 2026-09-01..2026-09-30 | publisher HM Revenue & Customs (HMRC)

## Directions and pages

### d1 [own_history] HMRC monthly release time series

Hypothesis: The target is a continuation of HMRC's published monthly seasonally adjusted series; recent months (mid-2026) and the same month in prior years (September 2023, 2024, 2025) anchor the level and seasonal factor. September typically shows a seasonal rise relative to August.

Queries issued by the searcher: HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 September 2026 provisional; UK residential property transactions seasonally adjusted monthly HMRC 2026 statistics; HMRC property transactions August 2026 seasonally adjusted 21 October 2026 release; UK residential transactions September 2025 seasonally adjusted HMRC 108,000; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above seasonally adjusted; HMRC property transactions September 2025 provisional seasonally adjusted monthly; HMRC property transactions September 2026 seasonally adjusted residential 95,000; "property transactions" HMRC September 2024 "92," residential seasonally adjusted provisional; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2025 provisional seasonally adjusted; HMRC property transactions statistics monthly seasonally adjusted table spreadsheet 2026; HMRC property transactions August 2026 provisional seasonally adjusted 96,710 July release September 2026; "UK monthly property transactions commentary" "August 2026" seasonally adjusted residential transactions provisional

### d2 [related_series] Bank of England / mortgage approvals and lending

Hypothesis: Mortgage approvals for house purchase lead completed transactions by roughly 1-3 months; approvals in July-August 2026 constrain September 2026 completions. A stable approvals-to-completions ratio (with cash share added) bridges to the target.

Queries issued by the searcher: Bank of England mortgage approvals house purchase August 2026 Money and Credit; UK mortgage approvals for house purchase 2026 monthly; HMRC monthly property transactions completed September 2026 provisional estimate; HMRC property transactions UK August 2026 seasonally adjusted £40,000; Bank of England mortgage approvals for house purchase monthly 2025 Money and Credit; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2025; Money and Credit August 2026 mortgage approvals house purchase Bank of England; "mortgage approvals" July 2026 Bank of England house purchase 65,000; HMRC property transactions August 2026 residential seasonally adjusted monthly figures; mortgage approvals to property transactions ratio UK cash buyers share transactions exceed approvals; Bank of England Money and Credit mortgage approvals house purchase August 2026; Bank of England mortgage approvals for house purchase 2026 number monthly; HMRC monthly property transactions UK September 2026 provisional seasonally adjusted; HMRC property transactions August 2026 residential £40,000 or above number

### d3 [related_series] Land Registry / Registers of Scotland / NI Land & Property Services completions

Hypothesis: Land Registry Price Paid data and devolved equivalents record completed sales; their monthly counts (England & Wales, Scotland, NI) sum to a UK total that approximates HMRC's coverage, with a lag and coverage adjustment.

Queries issued by the searcher: HMRC monthly property transactions completed in the UK with value of £40,000 or above September 2026; HM Land Registry Price Paid Data monthly transactions 2026; "monthly property transactions" commentary September 2026 seasonally adjusted residential HMRC 28 October 2026; HMRC UK property transactions August 2026 residential seasonally adjusted; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2026; HM Land Registry price paid data monthly transactions 2026; Registers of Scotland house sales monthly statistics 2026; Northern Ireland residential property sales statistics 2026 LPS; HMRC monthly property transactions completed in the UK with value of £40,000 or above September 2026; HM Land Registry price paid data monthly transactions 2026 England and Wales; Registers of Scotland monthly house sales statistics 2026 number of sales; Northern Ireland residential property sales statistics 2026 Land and Property Services quarterly

### d4 [related_series] RICS / surveyors and estate agents

Hypothesis: RICS residential market survey agreed sales and new buyer enquiries in July-September 2026 indicate the direction and magnitude of completed transactions relative to the prior year; a stable mapping from survey balances to HMRC volumes can be used.

Queries issued by the searcher: RICS residential market survey September 2026 agreed sales; RICS UK residential market survey August 2026 agreed sales new buyer enquiries; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 September 2026 provisional seasonally adjusted; HMRC property transactions August 2026 monthly estimate residential transactions seasonally adjusted; RICS residential market survey September 2026 agreed sales; RICS UK housing market survey August 2026 sales expectations; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 September 2026 provisional; HMRC property transactions August 2026 seasonally adjusted residential completions number; RICS residential market survey September 2026 agreed sales; RICS UK residential market survey August 2026 new buyer enquiries agreed sales; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK September 2026 provisional seasonally adjusted; HMRC property transactions August 2026 provisional seasonally adjusted 40,000 residential

### d7 [base_rates] Seasonal adjustment methodology

Hypothesis: HMRC's seasonally adjusted September figure equals the unadjusted September count times a September seasonal factor; estimating the unadjusted count from related series and applying the historical September factor yields the target.

### d8 [physical_flow] Conveyancing and completion pipeline

Hypothesis: Completions in September 2026 reflect sales agreed 2-4 months earlier; new-build completions and remortgage-free purchase completions can be approximated from housebuilder completion reports and conveyancer volumes, providing an independent level check.

### b1 [related_series] leading indicator

Hypothesis: A September 2026 HMRC figure can be anchored on the September 2025 level (95,980 SA) scaled by the year-on-year change implied by mid-2026 approvals and agreed-sales data. A clean, consistent approvals-to-completions ratio for 2025-2026 would let us convert the July-August 2026 approvals decline into a September 2026 completions change.

Queries issued by the searcher: Bank of England Money and Credit net mortgage approvals for house purchase 2026; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2025 seasonally adjusted; Bank of England Money and Credit August 2026 mortgage approvals house purchase; HMRC monthly property transactions commentary September 2026 residential seasonally adjusted 95,980; Bank of England Money and Credit net mortgage approvals house purchase 2026; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2025 95,980; Bank of England Money and Credit August 2026 mortgage approvals house purchases fell; "mortgage approvals" July 2026 Bank of England Money and Credit 57,000; Bank of England Money and Credit net mortgage approvals for house purchase 2025 2026 table; HMRC monthly property transactions completed UK value £40,000 or above September 2025 95,980 seasonally adjusted; HMRC residential property transactions August 2026 seasonally adjusted 30 September 2026; "property transactions" HMRC June 2026 seasonally adjusted residential figure

### b2 [own_history] seasonal factor

Hypothesis: The September seasonal factor (NSA/SA) is needed to convert an NSA estimate to the SA target. Historical September SA and NSA pairs (2023-2025) would pin the factor and reduce uncertainty in the seasonal-adjustment step.

## Methods (computed by code)

| id | method | estimate | inputs (points / assumptions) | backtest years | mean abs ln err | weight | weight basis | notes / error |

| m1 | formula_mc | 92,121 | ['p551'] / ['a1'] | [] |  | 0.50 | B1 of 2 blocks; geometric mean of blocks |  |
| m2 | formula_mc | 91,307 | ['p552'] / ['a2', 'a3'] | [] |  | 0.50 | B2 of 2 blocks; geometric mean of blocks |  |

- m1 coverage: the whole target
- m2 coverage: the whole target

Method details (computed by code):

- m1 formula_mc: p551 * a1; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 74,155 to 114,022; result unit (transaction/month) * 1 month converted to transaction; own 80% width x/÷1.38 (no error evidence, code's default width)
- m2 formula_mc: p552 * a3 * a2; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 60,840 to 137,197; result unit (transaction/month) * 1 month converted to transaction; own 80% width x/÷1.50 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- Combination: 2 block(s), equal weight per block (methods whose inputs overlap by at least half form one block; backtest errors set widths, not weights): B1 = m1: 92,121 (width x/÷1.38, no error evidence, code's default width); B2 = m2: 91,307 (width x/÷1.50, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs). Estimate = the geometric mean of the two blocks.
- Interval: root mean square of the block widths x/÷1.44; Birge ratio 0.02 (not above one, so no widening); final width x/÷1.44.

Result: central **91,713** transaction, 80% interval [63,601, 132,250] transaction, basis blocks (several routes with separate inputs: the width is the typical width of the routes' own intervals, not narrowed for their number), weight rule equal_blocks_geometric_mean, weight on methods that use assumptions 1.00

Unit of the estimate: transaction (transactions; total for 2026-09-01 to 2026-09-30)

## Published-value check (live use only)

## Final answer

The best estimate for UK residential property transactions of £[n] or above completed in September 2026, seasonally adjusted, as first provisionally reported by HMRC, is 91,713 transaction (80% model-based interval 63,601 to 132,250 transaction). This is a model-based range conditional on the stated assumptions, not a demonstrated accuracy guarantee.

Two routes were combined with equal weight per block: m1 gives 92,121 transaction (weight 50%) by applying a year-on-year factor to the September 2025 SA level of 95,980 transactions; m2 gives 91,307 transaction (weight 50%) by applying a y/y factor to the September 2025 NSA level of 102,420 transactions and then a September seasonal factor. The combination is a geometric mean because the two blocks share the September 2025 anchor, so their inputs overlap substantially.

Observed: the September 2025 levels. Assumed: the y/y factors (0.97, 0.97) and the seasonal factor (0.937). The main risk is a stamp-duty or Budget deadline pulling completions into September. Better evidence on September approvals and sales-agreed would most improve the estimate.

Unit of the estimate: transaction (transactions; total for 2026-09-01 to 2026-09-30).

Key assumptions (proposed by the analyst model, not observed; code samples each one between the bounds shown):
- a1 September 2026 year-on-year change factor in SA completions relative to September 2025: 0.97 [n], range 0.857 to 1.071 (range widened by code); used by m1. Overturned by: Evidence that September 2026 completions rose y/y (e.g. a late-summer surge or a stamp-duty deadline pulling sales forward).
- a2 September seasonal factor (SA/NSA) for 2026: 0.937 [n], range 0.6607 to 1.321 (range widened by code); used by m2. Overturned by: A change in HMRC's seasonal adjustment methodology or a September with an unusual within-year pattern.
- a3 September 2026 year-on-year change factor in NSA completions relative to September 2025: 0.97 [n], range 0.857 to 1.071 (range widened by code); used by m2. Overturned by: Evidence that September 2026 NSA completions were flat or up y/y.

Method details (computed by code):
- m1 formula_mc: p551 * a1; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 74,155 to 114,022; result unit (transaction/month) * 1 month converted to transaction; own 80% width x/÷1.38 (no error evidence, code's default width)
- m2 formula_mc: p552 * a3 * a2; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 60,840 to 137,197; result unit (transaction/month) * 1 month converted to transaction; own 80% width x/÷1.50 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- Combination: 2 block(s), equal weight per block (methods whose inputs overlap by at least half form one block; backtest errors set widths, not weights): B1 = m1: 92,121 (width x/÷1.38, no error evidence, code's default width); B2 = m2: 91,307 (width x/÷1.50, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs). Estimate = the geometric mean of the two blocks.
- Interval: root mean square of the block widths x/÷1.44; Birge ratio 0.02 (not above one, so no widening); final width x/÷1.44.
